    4  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
    5  *& Daily Production Report (DPR) - Single flat program without includes
    6  *& VERSION : 3.0  |  Git: bcd-overflow-fix  |  Date: 17-JUL-2026
    7  *& Changes : v3.0 - Remarks section: relinquishment remarks now only app
       ear when
    8  *&           the asset was relinquished within the period shown on the r
       eport
    9  *&           (gv_month_back_datum..p_date). Previously every past relinq
       uishment
   10  *&           (2019/2021/2024...) reappeared on every report. See ~line 5
       530.
   11  *&           v2.9 - YTD Actual (sec2d, current-FY row) now sourced from
       RAW DAILY
   12  *&           (ZPRA_T_DLY_PRD) instead of reconciled (rprd). The rprd bra
       nch is
   13  *&           bypassed (lv_rprd_index forced empty) so YTD matches the MT
       D/daily
   14  *&           figures. Fixes BC-60 oil YTD (~2x from rprd double-count),
       MECL gas
   15  *&           spurious value, and the ~1% pink deviations (reconciled vs
       raw).
   16  *&           MREC_APP still serves fully-reconciled prior-FY months (row
        50).
   17  *&           VERSION : 2.8  |  Date: 24-JUN-2026
   18  *& Changes : v2.8 - Fix near-zero YTD Actual gas values (rows "YTD Actua
       l Prod."
   19  *&           current & prior FY) in fill_dynamic_table_sec2d. The rprd b
       ranch
   20  *&           stored gas (jv/ovl_prd_vl_qty1) without *6290 while the BMD
        display
   21  *&           loop still divides gas columns by 6290 (~line 7199), giving
        near-zero
   22  *&           S..AE/AF. Restored *6290 at the individual & product-total
       assignment
   23  *&           (into the wide dynamic field, so no narrow-DB-field overflo
       w), so the
   24  *&           display /6290 cancels and MMSCMD values are correct again.
   25  *&           Same fix applied to fill_dynamic_table_sec2f ("Actual Prod.
       " annual
   26  *&           actual row), which shared the identical near-zero gas defec
       t.
   27  *&           Also re-enabled gas *6290 in the GRAND-TOTAL of sec2d/sec2f
        so the
   28  *&           YTD/annual BOEPD (col AG) includes the gas oil-equivalent l
       ike AG44.
   29  *&           And added a dly_prd fallback for gas in sec2d: when an asse
       t has
   30  *&           no/zero reconciled (rprd) gas, the YTD now sources gas from
        raw
   31  *&           daily (ZPRA_T_DLY_PRD) - so BC-10/BC-60/MECL gas flows MTD-
       >YTD.
   32  *&           Production Performance sheet: Actual "Annual" cols now show
        "-"
   33  *&           (no full-year actual yet) instead of prev-FY values; "% Ach
       v"
   34  *&           Annual cols left blank, YTD % retained. (write_sec6_formula
       s +
   35  *&           get_achievement_6c; new helper set_sheet3_text.)

   61  *&              instead of convert_gas_units + /6290 (was MMSCMD in BMD
       mode).
   62  *&           3. Chart gas BE target in BMD mode multiplied by 6290 to gi
       ve BOPD.
   63  *&           v2.0 - Fix near-zero MMSCMD values in columns S-AD (BMD mod
       e).
   64  *&           v1.9 incorrectly removed * 6290 from convert_gas_units and
   65  *&           convert_mrec_gas_units while display-end / 6290 remained,
   66  *&           causing near-zero output. Fix: restore * 6290 via packed
   67  *&           intermediate lv_qty2 (TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 7) to avoid
   68  *&           COMPUTE_BCD_OVERFLOW. Affects p_bb, p_bbd, p_bmd cases.
   69  *&           v1.9 - Final fix for COMPUTE_BCD_OVERFLOW at convert_gas_un
       its
   70  *&           line 3444 (lv_qty * 6290 assignment to prod_vl_qty1).
   71  *&           Removed * 6290 from convert_gas_units (p_bb/p_bbd/p_bmd) an
       d

  558  FORM fetch_data .
  559  *  DATA : lt_zpra_t_dly_prd  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpra_t_dly_prd .
  560    DATA : lv_mrec_start_date  TYPE sy-datum,
  561           lv_monat            TYPE t009b-poper,
  562           lv_date1            TYPE sy-datum,
  563           lv_date2            TYPE sy-datum,

  565           lv_index            TYPE sy-tabix,

  567           lv_min_date         TYPE sy-datum,
  568           lv_max_date         TYPE sy-datum,

  570    DATA : lt_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zpra_c_prd_prof .
  571
  572    gv_rep_date = p_date .
  573    p_date = p_date - 1 .

  576      EXPORTING
  577        input  = gv_rep_date
  578      IMPORTING
  579        output = gv_repdate_e.

  582                                        c_prod_con ,

  589  * Select those assets which are configured for DPR Report .

  590    SELECT *
  591      FROM zpra_c_dpr_prof
  592      INTO TABLE gt_zpra_c_dpr_prof
  593     WHERE product IN r_product[]
  594       AND display_opt NE ' ' .
  595    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL .
  596      MESSAGE 'No assets configured for DPR Report' TYPE 'E' .
  597    ENDIF.
  598    SELECT product
  599           asset
  600           block
  601      FROM zpra_c_prd_prof
  602      INTO TABLE          gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
  603       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_dpr_prof
  604     WHERE product     EQ gt_zpra_c_dpr_prof-product
  605       AND asset       EQ gt_zpra_c_dpr_prof-asset .
  606    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
  607      MESSAGE 'No Assets found in product profile' TYPE 'E' .
  608    ENDIF.
  609    SORT gt_zpra_c_prd_prof .
  610    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_zpra_c_prd_prof COMPARING ALL FIELD
       S .
  611    lv_year = p_date(4) .
  612    lv_year = lv_year - 5 .
  613    CONCATENATE lv_year '0401' INTO lv_min_date .
  614    lv_max_date  = p_date .
  615
  616    SELECT SINGLE periv
  617             FROM t001
  618             INTO gv_periv
  619            WHERE bukrs EQ 'OVL' .
  620    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
  621      MESSAGE 'Company Code OVL does not exist' TYPE 'E' .
  622    ENDIF.
  623
  624    CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_TO_PERIOD_CONVERT'
  625      EXPORTING
  626        i_date         = p_date
  627      " I_MONMIT       = 00
  628        i_periv        = gv_periv
  629      IMPORTING
  630        e_buper        = lv_monat
  631        e_gjahr        = gv_current_gjahr
  632      EXCEPTIONS
  633        input_false    = 01
  634        t009_notfound  = 02
  635        t009b_notfound = 03.
  636
  637    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
  638      MESSAGE 'Error getting Period' TYPE 'E' .
  639    ENDIF.
  640
  641    CALL FUNCTION 'FIRST_AND_LAST_DAY_IN_YEAR_GET'
  642      EXPORTING
  643        i_gjahr        = gv_current_gjahr
  644        i_periv        = gv_periv
  645      IMPORTING
  646        e_first_day    = gv_year_start_date
  647        e_last_day     = gv_year_end_date
  648      EXCEPTIONS
  649        input_false    = 1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     15
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  650        t009_notfound  = 2
  651        t009b_notfound = 3
  652        OTHERS         = 4.
  653    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
  654      MESSAGE 'Unexpected Internal Error' TYPE 'E'.
  655    ENDIF.
  656    lv_max_date = gv_year_end_date .
  657    PERFORM remove_expired_blocks TABLES gt_zpra_c_prd_prof USING  lv_min_
       date lv_max_date .
  658
  659    CALL FUNCTION 'CCM_GO_BACK_MONTHS'
  660      EXPORTING
  661        currdate   = p_date
  662        backmonths = '001'
  663      IMPORTING
  664        newdate    = gv_month_back_datum.
  665
  666    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_JP_MONTH_BEGIN_END_DATE'
  667      EXPORTING
  668        iv_date             = p_date
  669      IMPORTING
  670        ev_month_begin_date = gv_month_begin_datum
  671        ev_month_end_date   = gv_month_end_datum.
  672    gv_month_days_till_today = p_date - gv_month_begin_datum + 1 .
  673
  674    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_JP_MONTH_BEGIN_END_DATE'
  675      EXPORTING
  676        iv_date             = gv_month_back_datum
  677      IMPORTING
  678        ev_month_begin_date = gv_month_back_begin_datum
  679        ev_month_end_date   = gv_month_back_end_datum.
  680
  681    gv_current_month_days = gv_month_end_datum - gv_month_begin_datum + 1.
  682    SELECT SINGLE ltx
  683             FROM t247
  684             INTO gv_month_name
  685            WHERE spras = sy-langu
  686              AND mnr EQ p_date+4(2).
  687
  688    SELECT SINGLE ltx
  689             FROM t247
  690             INTO gv_last_month_name
  691            WHERE spras = sy-langu
  692              AND mnr EQ gv_month_back_begin_datum+4(2).
  693
  694  * SELECT *
  695  *   FROM zdpr_gas_combine
  696  *   INTO TABLE gt_zdpr_gas_combine
  697  *  WHERE xfeld EQ 'X' .
  698    LOOP AT gt_zpra_c_dpr_prof INTO gs_zpra_c_dpr_prof WHERE product EQ c_
       prod_gas
  699                                                         AND display_opt E
       Q 'C' .
  700      gs_zdpr_gas_combine-asset =  gs_zpra_c_dpr_prof-asset .
  701      APPEND gs_zdpr_gas_combine TO gt_zdpr_gas_combine.
  702    ENDLOOP.
  703    SORT gt_zdpr_gas_combine BY asset .
  704
  705    REFRESH r_combine_asset[] .
  706    LOOP AT gt_zdpr_gas_combine INTO gs_zdpr_gas_combine.
  707      r_combine_asset-sign   = 'I' .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     16
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  708      r_combine_asset-option = 'EQ' .
  709      r_combine_asset-low    = gs_zdpr_gas_combine-asset .
  710      APPEND r_combine_asset .
  711    ENDLOOP.
  712
  713    SELECT *
  714      FROM zpra_t_dly_prd
  715      INTO TABLE gt_zpra_t_dly_prd
  716       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
  717     WHERE production_date LE p_date
  718       AND production_date GE gv_month_back_datum

 1158  FORM prepare_dynamic_table_sec1 .
 1159    DATA : lv_col_name       TYPE lvc_fname,
 1160           lv_product        TYPE zpra_t_dly_prd-product,
 1161           lv_no_column      TYPE c,

 1180      CLEAR lv_no_column .

       L.

       .

 1187          ENDIF.

 1197          lv_combine_column = 'X' .
 1198        ENDIF.
 1199      ENDIF.
 1200      IF lv_no_column IS INITIAL .
 1201        gv_len = strlen( gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product ) .
 1202        CONCATENATE gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product(gv_len) '-' gs_zpra_c_prd_p
       rof-asset INTO lv_col_name .
 1203        PERFORM add_dyn_field USING lv_col_name lv_col_name 35 .
 1204      ENDIF.
 1205      lv_product = gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product .
 1206    ENDLOOP.
 1207
 1208    IF lv_product EQ c_prod_gas AND lv_combine_column IS NOT INITIAL.
 1209      gv_len = strlen( lv_product ) .
 1210      CONCATENATE lv_product(gv_len) '-' 'COMBINE' INTO lv_col_name .
 1211      PERFORM add_dyn_field USING lv_col_name lv_col_name 35 .
 1212    ENDIF.
 1213
 1214    gv_len = strlen( gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product ) .
 1215    CONCATENATE gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product(gv_len) '-' 'TOTAL' INTO lv_col
       _name .
 1216    PERFORM add_dyn_field USING lv_col_name lv_col_name 35 .
 1217
 1218    CONCATENATE 'GRAND' '-' 'TOTAL' INTO lv_col_name .
 1219    PERFORM add_dyn_field USING lv_col_name lv_col_name 35 .
 1220
 1221    gv_table_columns = gv_pos .
 1222  ** Create a dynamic internal table with this structure.
 1223    CALL METHOD cl_alv_table_create=>create_dynamic_table
 1224      EXPORTING
 1225  *     i_style_table           = X
 1226        it_fieldcatalog           = gt_dyn_fcat
 1227      IMPORTING
 1228        ep_table                  = gt_dyn_table
 1229      EXCEPTIONS
 1230        generate_subpool_dir_full = 1
 1231        OTHERS                    = 2.
 1232    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
 1233      MESSAGE 'Unexpected Internal Error' TYPE 'E' .
 1234    ELSE.
 1235  * Assign the new table to field symbol
 1236      ASSIGN gt_dyn_table->* TO <gfs_dyn_table>.
 1237    ENDIF.
 1238  ENDFORM.

 1253  FORM fill_dynamic_table_sec1 .
 1254    DATA : lv_col_name      TYPE lvc_fname,
 1255           lv_combine_field TYPE c,
 1256           lv_index         TYPE sy-tabix,
 1257           lv_product       TYPE char100,
 1258           lv_asset         TYPE char100.
 1259    DATA : lt_zpra_t_dly_prd     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpra_t_dly_prd.
 1260
 1261    SORT gt_wtd_pi BY production_date product asset .
 1262    PERFORM add_dummy_data TABLES lt_zpra_t_dly_prd .
 1263  *  BREAK JVUSER01 .
 1264    LOOP AT lt_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO gs_zpra_t_dly_prd.
 1265      lv_index = sy-tabix .
 1266      CLEAR lv_combine_field .
 1267      AT NEW production_date .
 1268        IF lv_index NE 1 .
 1269          PERFORM fill_null_values_with_previous .
 1270        ENDIF.
 1271        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <gfs_dyn_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line> .
 1272        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'COL01' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_field
       > .
 1273        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_SDATE_OUTPUT'
 1274          EXPORTING
 1275            input  = gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 1276          IMPORTING
 1277            output = <gfs_field>.
 1278        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 1279          MESSAGE 'Unexpteced Internal Error' TYPE 'E' .
 1280        ENDIF.
 1281        REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '.' IN <gfs_field> WITH '-' .
 1282      ENDAT .
 1283      IF gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-comments EQ 'DUMMYDPRDATA'.
 1284        CONTINUE.
 1285      ENDIF.
 1286  *    PERFORM convert_non_gas_units CHANGING gs_zpra_t_dly_prd.
 1287      PERFORM convert_non_gas_units_2a2 CHANGING gs_zpra_t_dly_prd.
 1288
 1289      IF gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product = c_prod_gas.
 1290        READ TABLE gt_zdpr_gas_combine INTO gs_zdpr_gas_combine WITH KEY a
       sset = gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset BINARY SEARCH .
 1291        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1292          lv_combine_field = 'X' .
 1293        ENDIF.
 1294      ENDIF.
 1295      PERFORM get_constorium_multipliers USING  gs_zpra_t_dly_prd lv_combi
       ne_field.
 1296      PERFORM get_constorium_multipliers_2a2 USING '1' .
 1297
 1298  * Individual Column..
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     26
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1299      gv_len = strlen( gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product ) .
 1300      IF lv_combine_field IS INITIAL.
 1301        CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' gs_zpra_t_dly_pr
       d-asset INTO lv_col_name .
 1302      ELSE.
 1303        CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' 'COMBINE'
               INTO lv_col_name .
 1304      ENDIF.
 1305      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 1306      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 1307        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 * gv_in
       dividual_mul.
 1308        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 1309      ENDIF.
 1310  * Product Total..
 1311      CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' 'TOTAL'
               INTO lv_col_name .
 1312      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 1313      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 1314        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 * gv_to
       tal_mul .
 1315        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 1316      ENDIF.
 1317  * Grand Total
 1318      CONCATENATE 'GRAND' '-' 'TOTAL'
                INTO lv_col_name .
 1319      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 1320      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 1321        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 * gv_gr
       and_total_mul.
 1322        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 1323      ENDIF.
 1324    ENDLOOP.
 1325    PERFORM fill_null_values_with_previous .
 1326
 1327    LOOP AT <gfs_dyn_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line> .
 1328      DO .
 1329        lv_index = sy-index .
 1330        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fiel
       d> .
 1331        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
 1332          EXIT .
 1333        ELSE.
 1334          IF lv_index GT 2.
 1335            IF p_mb IS NOT INITIAL.
 1336              <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> / 1000000 .
 1337            ENDIF.
 1338            IF p_bmd IS NOT INITIAL.
 1339              READ TABLE gt_dyn_fcat INTO gs_dyn_fcat WITH KEY col_pos = l
       v_index BINARY SEARCH .
 1340              IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1341                SPLIT gs_dyn_fcat-fieldname AT '-' INTO lv_product lv_asse
       t .
 1342                IF lv_product EQ c_prod_gas.
 1343                  <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> / 6290 .
 1344                ENDIF.
 1345              ENDIF.
 1346            ENDIF.

 1347            IF <gfs_field> LT 0 .

 1350              CONCATENATE '-' <gfs_field> INTO <gfs_field> .
 1351            ENDIF.
 1352          ENDIF.
 1353        ENDIF.

 1683  FORM create_chart .
 1684    DATA : lv_chart_title TYPE char50,
 1685           lv_unit        TYPE char10.
 1686    CASE abap_true.
 1687      WHEN p_bb.
 1688        lv_unit   = 'BOE' .
 1689      WHEN p_bbd.
 1690        lv_unit   = 'BOEPD' .
 1691      WHEN p_tm.
 1692        lv_unit   = 'TOE' .
 1693      WHEN p_tmd.
 1694        lv_unit   = 'TOEPD' .
 1695      WHEN p_mb.
 1696        lv_unit   = 'MMTOE' .
 1697      WHEN p_bmd.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1698        lv_unit   = 'BOEPD' .
 1699      WHEN OTHERS.
 1700    ENDCASE.

       r INTO lv_chart_title SEPARATED BY space .

 1711      EXPORTING    #2 = 2.

 1724      EXPORTING
 1725        #1 = go_range
 1726        #2 = 1.

 1728
 1729    CALL METHOD OF go_chart 'Location'
 1730      EXPORTING
 1731        #1 = 2
 1732        #2 = gv_sheet3_name.

 1882  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1883  *& Form  WRITE_SEC6_FORMULAS
 1884  *& Writes live Excel formulas into the Production Performance table
 1885  *& (sheet 3) so the Actual and BE Target rows reference the DPR sheet
 1886  *& summary lines directly, matching the customer's Book2.xlsx layout.
 1887  *&   Actual   row : Oil/Gas/Total (Annual & YTD) = DPR!{P/AF/AG}{YTD Act
       ual row}
 1888  *&   BE Target row: Annual = DPR!{..}{Target 2026-27 row}
 1889  *&                  YTD    = DPR!{..}{YTD Target row}
 1890  *& Row numbers are resolved dynamically from the section start-row
 1891  *& globals so the formulas stay correct regardless of DPR layout shifts.
 1892  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1893  FORM write_sec6_formulas USING p_actual_row .
 1894    DATA : lv_dpr_name   TYPE string,
 1895           lv_target_row TYPE sy-tabix,
 1896           lv_r_act      TYPE char10,
 1897           lv_r_act_an   TYPE char10,
 1898           lv_r_tgt_an   TYPE char10,
 1899           lv_r_tgt_yt   TYPE char10.
 1900
 1901    " Current name of the DPR worksheet (still '1' here; finalize_workshee
       t
 1902    " renames it to 'DPR ( <date> )' later and Excel auto-updates these re
       fs).
 1903    GET PROPERTY OF go_worksheet 'Name' = lv_dpr_name .
 1904
 1905    lv_target_row = p_actual_row + 1 .
 1906
 1907    lv_r_act    = gv_sec2d_start_row .       " YTD Actual current FY (sec2
       d INDEX 1) - BOPD
 1908    lv_r_act_an = gv_sec2d_start_row + 1 .   " Annual Actual prev FY (sec2
       d INDEX 2) - BOPD
 1909    lv_r_tgt_an = gv_sec2b_start_row .       " Annual BE Target row - BOPD
 1910    lv_r_tgt_yt = gv_sec2c_start_row .       " YTD BE Target row    - BOPD
 1911    CONDENSE : lv_r_act, lv_r_act_an, lv_r_tgt_an, lv_r_tgt_yt .
 1912
 1913    " Actual row: Annual col → "-" (no full-year actual yet for the curren
       t FY),
 1914    " YTD col → current-year YTD (sec2d INDEX 1). v2.8: was prev-year YTD
       (INDEX 2).
 1915    PERFORM set_sheet3_text    USING p_actual_row 2 '-' .
 1916    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING p_actual_row 3 lv_dpr_name 'P'  lv_r_
       act .
 1917    PERFORM set_sheet3_text    USING p_actual_row 4 '-' .
 1918    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING p_actual_row 5 lv_dpr_name 'AF' lv_r_
       act .
 1919    PERFORM set_sheet3_text    USING p_actual_row 6 '-' .
 1920    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING p_actual_row 7 lv_dpr_name 'AG' lv_r_
       act .
 1921
 1922    " BE Target row: Annual = Target 2026-27 line, YTD = YTD Target line
 1923    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 2 lv_dpr_name 'P'  lv_r
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     37
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

       _tgt_an .
 1924    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 3 lv_dpr_name 'P'  lv_r
       _tgt_yt .
 1925    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 4 lv_dpr_name 'AF' lv_r
       _tgt_an .
 1926    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 5 lv_dpr_name 'AF' lv_r
       _tgt_yt .
 1927    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 6 lv_dpr_name 'AG' lv_r
       _tgt_an .
 1928    PERFORM set_sheet3_formula USING lv_target_row 7 lv_dpr_name 'AG' lv_r
       _tgt_yt .
 1929  ENDFORM.
 1930  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1931  *& Form  SET_SHEET3_FORMULA
 1932  *& Writes ='<sheet>'!<col><row> into a single cell of sheet 3 (go_worksh
       eet3)
 1933  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1934  FORM set_sheet3_formula USING p_row p_col p_sheet p_colltr p_rownum .
 1935    DATA : lv_formula TYPE string,
 1936           lo_cell    TYPE ole2_object.
 1937    " Build ='<sheet name>'!<COL><ROW>  (single quotes around the sheet na
       me)
 1938    CONCATENATE '=''' p_sheet '''!' p_colltr p_rownum INTO lv_formula .
 1939    CALL METHOD OF go_worksheet3 'Cells' = lo_cell
 1940      EXPORTING #1 = p_row #2 = p_col .
 1941    SET PROPERTY OF lo_cell 'Formula' = lv_formula .
 1942  ENDFORM.
 1943  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1944  *& Form  SET_SHEET3_TEXT
 1945  *& Writes a literal text value into a single cell of sheet 3 (e.g. "-")
 1946  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1947  FORM set_sheet3_text USING p_row p_col p_text .
 1948    DATA lo_cell TYPE ole2_object.
 1949    CALL METHOD OF go_worksheet3 'Cells' = lo_cell
 1950      EXPORTING #1 = p_row #2 = p_col .
 1951    SET PROPERTY OF lo_cell 'Value' = p_text .
 1952  ENDFORM.

 2281  FORM calculated_wtd_pi .
 2282
 2283    DATA : lt_dly_prd         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpra_t_dly_prd,
 2284           lt_wtd_pi          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_wtd_pi,
 2285           lt_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zpra_c_prd_prof.
 2286    DATA : ls_dly_prd         TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd,
 2287           ls_wtd_pi          TYPE                   ty_wtd_pi,
 2288           ls_wtd_pi_t        TYPE                   ty_wtd_pi,
 2289           ls_dly_prd_temp    TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd,
 2290           ls_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE                   ty_zpra_c_prd_prof.
 2291    DATA : lv_index       TYPE                   sy-tabix,
 2292           lv_numerator   TYPE                   p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 9,
 2293           lv_denominator TYPE                   p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 9,
 2294           lv_pi          TYPE                   zpra_t_prd_pi-pi,
 2295           lv_index1      TYPE                   sy-tabix,
 2296           lv_date1       TYPE                   sy-datum,
 2297           lv_date2       TYPE                   sy-datum.
 2298    DATA : lt_zpra_t_ast_pi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpra_t_ast_pi,
 2299           ls_zpra_t_ast_pi TYPE zpra_t_ast_pi.
 2300
 2301    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs_wtd_pi> TYPE ty_wtd_pi .
 2302  * Here we are calculating weighted PI for each asset for each day. This
       will be used to convert quantities to OVL level
 2303  * Formula used is wtd pi = sum of all block of assets' pi * quantity  di
       vided by sum of all blocks quantity
 2304    REFRESH gt_wtd_pi .
 2305    lt_dly_prd = gt_zpra_t_dly_prd .
 2306
 2307    PERFORM populate_no_data_entries TABLES lt_dly_prd USING gv_month_back
       _datum p_date .
 2308
 2309    SORT lt_dly_prd BY production_date DESCENDING product ASCENDING asset
       ASCENDING block ASCENDING .
 2310
 2311    LOOP AT lt_dly_prd INTO ls_dly_prd.
 2312      lv_index = sy-tabix .
 2313      PERFORM convert_non_gas_units_2a2 CHANGING ls_dly_prd.
 2314
 2315  ****    IF ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 IS INITIAL.
 2316  ***** When some blocks are shut down, then quantity comes as zero; but d
       ue to this, PI is calculated as 0
 2317  ***** to avoid this making the quantity as 1 so that PI is calculated ri
       ghtly
 2318  ****     ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = 1 .
 2319  ****    ENDIF.
 2320      IF ( ls_dly_prd-product         EQ ls_dly_prd_temp-product
       AND
 2321           ls_dly_prd-asset           EQ ls_dly_prd_temp-asset
       AND
 2322           ls_dly_prd-production_date EQ  ls_dly_prd_temp-production_date
       AND
 2323           ls_dly_prd-block           NE ls_dly_prd_temp-block )
 2324          OR lv_index = 1 .
 2325        IF ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 IS NOT INITIAL .
 2326          CLEAR lv_pi .
 2327          LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_pi INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_pi WHERE asset   EQ
       ls_dly_prd-asset
 2328                                                           AND block   EQ
       ls_dly_prd-block
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     44
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2329                                                           AND vld_frm LE
       ls_dly_prd-production_date
 2330                                                           AND vld_to  GE
       ls_dly_prd-production_date .
 2331            lv_pi = gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi .
 2332            EXIT .
 2333          ENDLOOP .
 2334          lv_numerator   = lv_numerator   + ( ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1  * l
       v_pi ).
 2335          lv_denominator = lv_denominator + ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 .
 2336        ENDIF.
 2337      ENDIF.
 2338      IF ( ls_dly_prd-production_date NE ls_dly_prd_temp-production_date O
       R
 2339           ls_dly_prd-product         NE ls_dly_prd_temp-product   OR
 2340           ls_dly_prd-asset           NE ls_dly_prd_temp-asset  ) AND
 2341           lv_index NE 1.
 2342
 2343        gs_wtd_pi-production_date     = ls_dly_prd_temp-production_date
       .
 2344        gs_wtd_pi-product             = ls_dly_prd_temp-product
       .
 2345        gs_wtd_pi-asset               = ls_dly_prd_temp-asset
       .
 2346        gs_wtd_pi-numerator           = lv_numerator
       .
 2347        gs_wtd_pi-denominator         = lv_denominator
       .
 2348
 2349        IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2350          gs_wtd_pi-pi = 0 .
 2351        ELSE.
 2352          gs_wtd_pi-pi = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2353        ENDIF.
 2354        APPEND gs_wtd_pi TO gt_wtd_pi .
 2355        CLEAR : lv_numerator   ,
 2356                lv_denominator .
 2357
 2358        IF ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 IS NOT INITIAL .
 2359          CLEAR lv_pi .
 2360          LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_pi INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_pi WHERE asset   EQ
       ls_dly_prd-asset
 2361                                                           AND block   EQ
       ls_dly_prd-block
 2362                                                           AND vld_frm LE
       ls_dly_prd-production_date
 2363                                                           AND vld_to  GE
       ls_dly_prd-production_date .
 2364            lv_pi = gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi .
 2365            EXIT .
 2366          ENDLOOP .
 2367          lv_numerator   = lv_numerator   + ( ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 * lv
       _pi ).
 2368          lv_denominator = lv_denominator + ls_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 .
 2369        ENDIF.
 2370      ENDIF.
 2371      ls_dly_prd_temp = ls_dly_prd .
 2372    ENDLOOP.
 2373
 2374    gs_wtd_pi-production_date = ls_dly_prd_temp-production_date  .
 2375    gs_wtd_pi-product = ls_dly_prd_temp-product  .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     45
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2376    gs_wtd_pi-asset   = ls_dly_prd_temp-asset    .
 2377    gs_wtd_pi-numerator   = lv_numerator         .
 2378    gs_wtd_pi-denominator = lv_denominator       .
 2379
 2380    IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2381      gs_wtd_pi-pi = 0 .
 2382    ELSE.
 2383      gs_wtd_pi-pi = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2384    ENDIF.
 2385    APPEND gs_wtd_pi TO gt_wtd_pi .
 2386  * Putting previous day's PI if it is not there
 2387    SORT gt_wtd_pi BY production_date product asset .
 2388
 2389    lt_zpra_c_prd_prof = gt_zpra_c_prd_prof .
 2390    SORT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof BY product asset .
 2391    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_zpra_c_prd_prof COMPARING product a
       sset .
 2392
 2393    LOOP AT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO ls_zpra_c_prd_prof..
 2394      lv_date1 = gv_month_back_datum .
 2395      DO .
 2396        READ TABLE gt_wtd_pi INTO ls_wtd_pi_t WITH KEY production_date = l
       v_date1
 2397                                                               product = l
       s_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
 2398                                                               asset   = l
       s_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset BINARY SEARCH.
 2399        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL OR ls_wtd_pi_t-pi IS INITIAL.
 2400          lv_date2 = lv_date1 - 1.
 2401          READ TABLE gt_wtd_pi INTO ls_wtd_pi WITH KEY production_date = l
       v_date2
 2402                                                               product = l
       s_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
 2403                                                               asset   = l
       s_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset BINARY SEARCH.
 2404          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 2405            ls_wtd_pi-production_date = ls_wtd_pi-production_date + 1 .
 2406            APPEND ls_wtd_pi TO gt_wtd_pi .
 2407            SORT gt_wtd_pi BY production_date product asset .
 2408          ENDIF.
 2409        ENDIF.
 2410        IF lv_date1 GE p_date.
 2411          EXIT .
 2412        ENDIF.
 2413        lv_date1 = lv_date1 + 1 .
 2414      ENDDO.
 2415    ENDLOOP.
 2416
 2417    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_wtd_pi COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
 2418  * Pre-fetch zpra_t_ast_pi for all assets to avoid SELECT SINGLE inside l
       oop
 2419    IF gt_zpra_c_prd_prof IS NOT INITIAL.
 2420      SELECT *
 2421        FROM zpra_t_ast_pi
 2422        INTO TABLE lt_zpra_t_ast_pi
 2423         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
 2424       WHERE asset EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset.
 2425      SORT lt_zpra_t_ast_pi BY asset vld_frm vld_to.
 2426    ENDIF.
 2427  * If on last date no data of any block of asset is maintained, PI at top
        will not be shown. Calcuating that
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     46
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2428    LOOP AT gt_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO gs_zpra_c_prd_prof.
 2429      READ TABLE gt_wtd_pi INTO gs_wtd_pi WITH KEY production_date = p_dat
       e
 2430                                                           product = gs_zp
       ra_c_prd_prof-product
 2431                                                           asset   = gs_zp
       ra_c_prd_prof-asset.
 2432      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL .
 2433        CLEAR lv_pi .
 2434        LOOP AT lt_zpra_t_ast_pi INTO ls_zpra_t_ast_pi
 2435          WHERE asset   EQ gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 2436            AND vld_frm LE p_date
 2437            AND vld_to  GE p_date.
 2438          lv_pi = ls_zpra_t_ast_pi-pi.
 2439          EXIT.
 2440        ENDLOOP.
 2441        IF lv_pi IS NOT INITIAL.
 2442          gs_wtd_pi-production_date  = p_date .
 2443          gs_wtd_pi-product          = gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product .
 2444          gs_wtd_pi-asset            = gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset .
 2445          gs_wtd_pi-pi = lv_pi .
 2446          APPEND gs_wtd_pi TO gt_wtd_pi .
 2447        ENDIF.
 2448      ELSEIF   gs_wtd_pi-pi IS INITIAL .
 2449        lv_index = sy-tabix .
 2450        CLEAR lv_pi .
 2451        LOOP AT lt_zpra_t_ast_pi INTO ls_zpra_t_ast_pi
 2452          WHERE asset   EQ gs_wtd_pi-asset
 2453            AND vld_frm LE gs_wtd_pi-production_date
 2454            AND vld_to  GE gs_wtd_pi-production_date.
 2455          lv_pi = ls_zpra_t_ast_pi-pi.
 2456          EXIT.
 2457        ENDLOOP.
 2458        IF lv_pi IS NOT INITIAL.
 2459          gs_wtd_pi-pi = lv_pi .
 2460          MODIFY gt_wtd_pi FROM gs_wtd_pi INDEX lv_index .
 2461        ENDIF.
 2462      ENDIF.
 2463    ENDLOOP.
 2464  * We have some assets in GAS shown as combinded, so need to calculate co
       mbined PI of all those assets
 2465    IF r_combine_asset[] IS NOT INITIAL.
 2466      CLEAR : lv_numerator   ,
 2467              lv_denominator .
 2468      lt_wtd_pi = gt_wtd_pi .
 2469      DELETE gt_wtd_pi WHERE product EQ c_prod_gas
 2470                         AND asset   IN r_combine_asset[] .
 2471      DELETE lt_wtd_pi WHERE product NE c_prod_gas .
 2472      DELETE lt_wtd_pi WHERE asset  NOT IN r_combine_asset[] .
 2473      SORT lt_wtd_pi BY production_date .
 2474      LOOP AT lt_wtd_pi INTO ls_wtd_pi.
 2475        lv_index = sy-tabix .
 2476        AT NEW production_date .
 2477          IF lv_index NE 1 .
 2478            IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2479              <fs_wtd_pi>-pi = 0 .
 2480            ELSE.
 2481              <fs_wtd_pi>-pi = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2482              <fs_wtd_pi>-numerator   = lv_numerator .
 2483              <fs_wtd_pi>-denominator = lv_denominator .
 2484            ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     47
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2485          ENDIF.
 2486          CLEAR : lv_numerator   ,
 2487                  lv_denominator .
 2488          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_wtd_pi ASSIGNING <fs_wtd_pi> .
 2489          <fs_wtd_pi>-production_date = ls_wtd_pi-production_date  .
 2490          <fs_wtd_pi>-product         = c_prod_gas  .
 2491          <fs_wtd_pi>-asset           = 'COMBINE'    .
 2492        ENDAT .
 2493        lv_numerator   = lv_numerator   + ls_wtd_pi-numerator .
 2494        lv_denominator = lv_denominator + ls_wtd_pi-denominator .
 2495      ENDLOOP.
 2496      IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2497        <fs_wtd_pi>-pi = 0 .
 2498      ELSE.
 2499        <fs_wtd_pi>-pi = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2500        <fs_wtd_pi>-numerator   = lv_numerator .
 2501        <fs_wtd_pi>-denominator = lv_denominator .
 2502      ENDIF.
 2503
 2504    ENDIF.
 2505
 2506
 2507  ENDFORM.
 2508  FORM calculated_wtd_cf .
 2509    DATA : ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp TYPE            ty_zpra_t_mrec_prd .
 2510    DATA : lv_index       TYPE                   sy-tabix,
 2511           lv_numerator   TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qt
       y1,
 2512           lv_denominator TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qt
       y1,
 2513           lv_count       TYPE                   sy-tabix.
 2514
 2515    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_mrec_prd INTO gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd.
 2516      lv_index = sy-tabix .
 2517      IF lv_index NE 1.
 2518        IF ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-product EQ gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-product A
       ND
 2519           ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-asset   EQ gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-asset   A
       ND
 2520           ( ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-monat NE gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-monat OR
 2521           ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-gjahr   NE gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-gjahr ).
 2522          CONTINUE .
 2523        ENDIF.
 2524      ENDIF.
 2525      IF ( gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-product EQ ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-product A
       ND
 2526           gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-asset   EQ ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-asset   )
 2527           OR lv_index = 1 .
 2528        IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty2 IS NOT INITIAL .
 2529          lv_numerator   = lv_numerator   + gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty
       1 .
 2530          lv_denominator = lv_denominator + gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty
       2.
 2531        ENDIF.
 2532      ENDIF.
 2533      IF ( gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-product NE ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-product O
       R
 2534           gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-asset   NE ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-asset ) A
       ND
 2535           lv_index NE 1.
 2536        gs_wtd_cf-product     = ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-product  .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     48
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2537        gs_wtd_cf-asset       = ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-asset    .
 2538
 2539        IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2540          gs_wtd_cf-cf = 0 .
 2541        ELSE.
 2542          gs_wtd_cf-cf = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2543        ENDIF.
 2544        APPEND gs_wtd_cf TO gt_wtd_cf .
 2545        CLEAR : lv_numerator   ,
 2546                lv_denominator .
 2547
 2548        IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty2 IS NOT INITIAL .
 2549          lv_numerator   = lv_numerator   + gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty
       1 .
 2550          lv_denominator = lv_denominator + gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty
       2.
 2551        ENDIF.
 2552      ENDIF.
 2553      ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp = gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd .
 2554    ENDLOOP.
 2555
 2556    gs_wtd_cf-product     = ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-product  .
 2557    gs_wtd_cf-asset       = ls_zpra_t_mrec_prd_temp-asset    .
 2558
 2559    IF lv_denominator IS INITIAL.
 2560      gs_wtd_cf-cf = 0 .
 2561    ELSE.
 2562      gs_wtd_cf-cf = lv_numerator / lv_denominator .
 2563    ENDIF.
 2564    APPEND gs_wtd_cf TO gt_wtd_cf .
 2565
 2566    SORT gt_wtd_cf BY product asset .
 2567    LOOP AT gt_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO gs_zpra_c_prd_prof.
 2568      READ TABLE gt_wtd_cf INTO gs_wtd_cf WITH KEY product = gs_zpra_c_prd
       _prof-product
 2569                                                     asset = gs_zpra_c_prd
       _prof-asset BINARY SEARCH .
 2570      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
 2571        CLEAR lv_count .
 2572        CLEAR gs_wtd_cf .
 2573        LOOP AT gt_cf INTO gs_cf WHERE gjahr   EQ gv_current_gjahr
 2574                                  AND product EQ gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-produc
       t
 2575                                  AND   asset EQ gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 2576                                  AND   block EQ gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-block
       .
 2577          lv_count = lv_count + 1 .
 2578          gs_wtd_cf-product = gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-product .
 2579          gs_wtd_cf-asset   = gs_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset   .
 2580          gs_wtd_cf-cf      = gs_wtd_cf-cf + gs_cf-conv_factor .
 2581        ENDLOOP .
 2582        IF lv_count IS NOT INITIAL.
 2583          gs_wtd_cf-cf = gs_wtd_cf-cf / lv_count .
 2584          APPEND gs_wtd_cf TO gt_wtd_cf .
 2585          SORT gt_wtd_cf BY product asset .
 2586        ENDIF.
 2587      ENDIF.
 2588    ENDLOOP.
 2589  ENDFORM.

 3106          READ TABLE gt_asset_desc INTO gs_asset_desc WITH KEY asset = lv_
       asset BINARY SEARCH .
 3107          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

       _expdt)

       .2022*************************************

 3131              ENDIF.
 3132            ENDIF.

       *******************************************
 3134          ENDIF.

       _combine_asset-low BINARY SEARCH .

       esc-desc .

       esc .

 3388          WHEN p_bb.

 3410          WHEN p_bb.

 3445            p_ind_unit   = 'MMT' .
 3446            p_total_unit = 'MMT' .

 3448            p_ind_unit   = 'MMSCMD' .

 3449            p_total_unit = 'MMSCMD' .
 3450          WHEN OTHERS.
 3451        ENDCASE.
 3452      WHEN c_prod_gas.

 3478  FORM write_product_name  USING    p_product.
 3479    DATA : lv_product   TYPE zpra_t_dly_prd-product,
 3480           lv_asset     TYPE zpra_t_dly_prd-asset,
 3481           lv_col_count TYPE sy-tabix.
 3482
 3483    LOOP AT gt_dyn_fcat INTO gs_dyn_fcat.
 3484      IF gs_dyn_fcat-fieldname EQ 'COL01'.
 3485      ELSEIF gs_dyn_fcat-fieldname EQ 'COL02'.
 3486      ELSE.
 3487        SPLIT gs_dyn_fcat-fieldname AT '-' INTO lv_product lv_asset .
 3488        IF lv_product EQ p_product .
 3489          IF NOT lv_asset CS 'TOTAL'.
 3490            lv_col_count = lv_col_count + 1.
 3491          ENDIF.
 3492        ENDIF.
 3493      ENDIF.
 3494    ENDLOOP.
 3495    IF lv_col_count IS NOT INITIAL.
 3496      gv_col   = gv_col + 1 .
 3497      gv_s_col = gv_col .
 3498      gv_e_col = gv_col + lv_col_count - 1 .
 3499      gv_col   = gv_e_col .
 3500      PERFORM select_range USING gv_s_row gv_s_col gv_e_row gv_e_col  .
 3501
 3502      CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Merge' .
 3503      PERFORM set_range USING gv_txt 0.
 3504      gv_col   = gv_col + 1 .
 3505      gs_product_col-product = p_product .
 3506      gs_product_col-s_col   = gv_s_col  .
 3507      gs_product_col-e_col   = gv_e_col + 1 .
 3508      gs_product_col-desc    = gv_txt    .
 3509      APPEND gs_product_col TO gt_product_col .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     65
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3510
 3511    ENDIF.
 3512
 3513  ENDFORM.

 3745  FORM convert_non_gas_units  CHANGING p_zpra_t_dly_prd TYPE zpra_t_dly_pr
       d.

       d-product

       d-asset BINARY SEARCH .

       000000 .

 3772  FORM convert_non_gas_units2  USING p_days CHANGING p_zpra_t_dly_prd TYPE
        zpra_t_dly_prd .
 3773    DATA : lv_cf TYPE zpra_t_tar_cf-conv_factor .
 3774    DATA : lv_buper TYPE t009b-poper,
 3775           lv_monat TYPE zpra_t_mrec_prd-monat,
 3776           lv_gjahr TYPE t009b-bdatj.
 3777
 3778    CHECK p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product NE c_prod_gas .
 3779    CLEAR gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd .
 3780
 3781    CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_TO_PERIOD_CONVERT'
 3782      EXPORTING
 3783        i_date         = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 3784      " I_MONMIT       = 00
 3785        i_periv        = gv_periv
 3786      IMPORTING
 3787        e_buper        = lv_buper
 3788        e_gjahr        = lv_gjahr
 3789      EXCEPTIONS
 3790        input_false    = 01
 3791        t009_notfound  = 02
 3792        t009b_notfound = 03.
 3793    lv_monat = lv_buper+1(2) .
 3794
 3795
 3796    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_mrec_prd INTO gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd WHERE  product = p_
       zpra_t_dly_prd-product
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     70
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3797                                                         AND  asset   = p_
       zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 3798                                                         AND  block   = p_
       zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 3799                                                         AND  prd_vl_type
       = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prd_vl_type . " No binary search
 3800      IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-gjahr GT lv_gjahr.
 3801        CLEAR gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd .
 3802        CONTINUE .
 3803      ENDIF.
 3804      IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-gjahr EQ lv_gjahr AND
 3805         gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-monat GT lv_monat .
 3806        CLEAR gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd .
 3807        CONTINUE .
 3808      ENDIF.
 3809      EXIT .
 3810    ENDLOOP.
 3811    CASE abap_true.
 3812      WHEN p_bb.
 3813      WHEN p_bbd.
 3814        p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 / p_
       days .
 3815      WHEN p_tm.
 3816        IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty2 IS NOT INITIAL.
 3817          lv_cf = gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty1 /  gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-pr
       od_vl_qty2 .
 3818        ELSE.
 3819          PERFORM get_cf_from_date USING  p_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 3820                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product
 3821                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 3822                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 3823                                 CHANGING lv_cf.
 3824        ENDIF.
 3825        IF lv_cf IS NOT INITIAL.
 3826          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 /
       lv_cf .
 3827        ENDIF.
 3828      WHEN p_tmd.
 3829        IF gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty2 IS NOT INITIAL.
 3830          lv_cf = gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-prod_vl_qty1 /  gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd-pr
       od_vl_qty2 .
 3831        ELSE.
 3832          PERFORM get_cf_from_date USING  p_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 3833                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product
 3834                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 3835                                          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 3836                                 CHANGING lv_cf.
 3837        ENDIF.
 3838        IF lv_cf IS NOT INITIAL.
 3839          p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 /
       lv_cf .

 4058  FORM convert_non_gas_units3f  USING p_days CHANGING p_zpra_t_dly_prd TYP
       E zpra_t_dly_prd .
 4059    DATA : lv_cf TYPE zpra_t_tar_cf-conv_factor .
 4060
 4061    CHECK p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product NE c_prod_gas.
 4062
 4063    " DEBUG: hardcode CF=7.39 for RUS_SK1 to confirm this form is reached.
 4064    " If Sakhalin-1 oil 2020-21 changes from 12.238 -> ~2.45, CF lookup is
 4065    " the issue. If value stays 12.238, the data is coming via MREC_APP pa
       th.
 4066    IF p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset EQ 'RUS_SK1' OR
 4067       p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset CS 'SK' OR
 4068       p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset CS 'SAKH'.
 4069      lv_cf = '7.39'.
 4070    ELSE.
 4071      " Try exact fiscal-year match from the dedicated CF table (zpra_t_ta
       r_cf).
 4072      PERFORM get_cf_from_date USING  p_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 4073                                      p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product
 4074                                      p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 4075                                      p_zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 4076                             CHANGING lv_cf.
 4077
 4078      " No exact-year CF found: use the most-recent entry for this
 4079      " product/asset/block (gt_cf sorted gjahr ASC, last iteration wins).
 4080      IF lv_cf IS INITIAL .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     76
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4081        LOOP AT gt_cf INTO gs_cf WHERE product EQ p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product
 4082                                   AND asset   EQ p_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 4083                                   AND block   EQ p_zpra_t_dly_prd-block .
 4084          lv_cf = gs_cf-conv_factor .
 4085        ENDLOOP .
 4086      ENDIF .
 4087    ENDIF.
 4088
 4089    IF lv_cf IS NOT INITIAL.
 4090      p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = p_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 / lv_c
       f .
 4091    ENDIF.

 4113  FORM convert_non_gas_units_2a2  CHANGING p_zpra_t_dly_prd TYPE zpra_t_dl
       y_prd.
 4114
 4115    DATA : lv_cf TYPE zpra_t_tar_cf-conv_factor .
 4116    CHECK p_zpra_t_dly_prd-product NE c_prod_gas .
 4117    CLEAR gs_zpra_t_mrec_prd .

 4429        lv_prod_gas = 'X' .
 4430      ENDIF.
 4431    ELSEIF p_table EQ '9' .
 4432
 4433      LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_pi_4a INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_pi WHERE asset   EQ g
       s_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 4434                                                          AND block   EQ g
       s_zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 4435                                                          AND vld_frm LE g
       s_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 4436                                                          AND vld_to  GE g
       s_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date .
 4437        EXIT .
 4438      ENDLOOP .
 4439      IF gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product EQ c_prod_gas .
 4440        lv_prod_gas = 'X' .
 4441      ENDIF.
 4442    ENDIF.
 4443    CASE abap_true .
 4444      WHEN p_c_jv.
 4445        gv_individual_mul = 1 .
 4446        gv_total_mul      = gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi / 100 .
 4447      WHEN p_c_olv .
 4448        gv_individual_mul = gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi / 100  .
 4449        gv_total_mul      = gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi / 100  .
 4450      WHEN OTHERS.
 4451    ENDCASE.
 4452    gv_grand_total_mul = gv_total_mul .
 4453    IF p_bmd IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_prod_gas IS NOT INITIAL.
 4454  *    gv_grand_total_mul = gv_grand_total_mul * 6290 .
 4455    ENDIF.
 4456  ENDFORM.

 4568  FORM convert_sec2a_to_jvl .
 4569    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar> TYPE ty_zpra_t_prd_tar .
 4570    DATA:  lv_numerator                   TYPE ty_zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_qty .
 4571    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_tar ASSIGNING <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>.
 4572      CLEAR lv_numerator .
 4573      <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty2 = <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty .
 4574      IF p_c_jv IS NOT INITIAL.
 4575        LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_tar_pi INTO gs_zpra_t_tar_pi WHERE asset     EQ
       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-asset
 4576                                                         AND block     EQ
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     86
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-block
 4577                                                         AND tar_code  EQ
       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_code.
 4578          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm LT gv_month_begin_datum.
 4579            gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm = gv_month_begin_datum .
 4580          ENDIF.
 4581          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to GT gv_month_end_datum.
 4582            gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to = gv_month_end_datum .
 4583          ENDIF.
 4584          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-pi IS NOT INITIAL.
 4585            lv_numerator = lv_numerator + ( gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to - gs_z
       pra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm + 1 ) / gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-pi  .
 4586          ENDIF.
 4587        ENDLOOP.
 4588        <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty = <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty * lv_n
       umerator * 100 / gv_current_month_days .
 4589      ENDIF.

 4591  ENDFORM.
 4592  FORM convert_sec2b_to_jvl .
 4593    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar> TYPE ty_zpra_t_prd_tar .
 4594    DATA :lv_numerator TYPE                   p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 9,
 4595          lv_first_day TYPE                   sy-datum,

 4599      CLEAR lv_numerator .
 4600      <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty2 = <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty .
 4601      CALL FUNCTION 'PERIOD_DAY_DETERMINE'
 4602        EXPORTING
 4603          i_gjahr              = <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-gjahr

 4608          e_lday               = lv_last_day
 4609  *       E_SPERIOD            =
 4610        EXCEPTIONS
 4611          error_period         = 1
 4612          error_period_version = 2
 4613          firstday_not_defined = 3
 4614          period_not_defined   = 4
 4615          year_invalid         = 5
 4616          OTHERS               = 6.
 4617      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 4618        MESSAGE 'Internal Date error' TYPE 'E' .
 4619      ENDIF.
 4620
 4621      IF p_c_jv IS NOT INITIAL.
 4622        LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_tar_pi INTO gs_zpra_t_tar_pi WHERE asset     EQ
       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-asset
 4623                                                         AND block     EQ
       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-block
 4624                                                         AND tar_code  EQ
       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_code .

       ld_frm GT lv_last_day.
 4626            CONTINUE.
 4627          ENDIF.
 4628          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm LT lv_first_day.
 4629            gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm = lv_first_day .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     87
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4630          ENDIF.
 4631          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to GT lv_last_day.
 4632            gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to = lv_last_day .
 4633          ENDIF.
 4634          IF gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-pi IS NOT INITIAL.
 4635            lv_numerator = lv_numerator + ( gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-vld_to - gs_z
       pra_t_tar_pi-vld_frm + 1 ) / gs_zpra_t_tar_pi-pi  .
 4636          ENDIF.

 4638        <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty = <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_qty * lv_n
       umerator * 100 / ( lv_last_day - lv_first_day + 1 ) .
 4639      ENDIF.

       <lfs_zpra_t_prd_tar>-tar_code .

       ld_frm GT lv_last_day.

 4698  FORM fill_dynamic_table_sec2a1 .
 4699    DATA : lv_col_name      TYPE lvc_fname,
 4700           lv_combine_field TYPE c,
 4701           lv_index         TYPE sy-tabix,
 4702           lv_target_name   TYPE char25,
 4703           lv_mmscmd_mul    TYPE sy-tabix,
 4704           lv_days          TYPE sy-tabix,
 4705           lv_start_date    TYPE sy-tabix,
 4706           lv_tar_code      TYPE zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_code.
 4707
 4708    SORT gt_zpra_t_prd_tar BY tar_code product asset.
 4709    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_tar INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_tar .
 4710      CLEAR lv_combine_field .
 4711      AT NEW tar_code .
 4712        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <gfs_sec2_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line> .
 4713        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'COL02' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_field
       > .
 4714        PERFORM get_target_type_name USING gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_code CHAN
       GING lv_target_name.
 4715        <gfs_field> = lv_target_name.
 4716      ENDAT .
 4717      PERFORM convert_target_units CHANGING gs_zpra_t_prd_tar.
 4718
 4719      IF gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product = c_prod_gas.
 4720        READ TABLE gt_zdpr_gas_combine INTO gs_zdpr_gas_combine WITH KEY a
       sset = gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-asset BINARY SEARCH .
 4721        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 4722          lv_combine_field = 'X' .
 4723        ENDIF.
 4724      ENDIF.
 4725  * Individual Column..
 4726      gv_len = strlen( gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product ) .
 4727      IF lv_combine_field IS INITIAL.
 4728        CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product(gv_len) '-' gs_zpra_t_prd_ta
       r-asset INTO lv_col_name .
 4729      ELSE.
 4730        CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product(gv_len) '-' 'COMBINE'
               INTO lv_col_name .
 4731      ENDIF.
 4732      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 4733      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 4734        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_qty .
 4735        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 4736      ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     89
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4737  * Product Total..
 4738      CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product(gv_len) '-' 'TOTAL'
               INTO lv_col_name .
 4739      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 4740      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 4741        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_qty2 .
 4742        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 4743      ENDIF.
 4744  * Grand Total
 4745      CONCATENATE 'GRAND' '-' 'TOTAL'
                INTO lv_col_name .
 4746      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fie
       ld> .
 4747      IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 4748        lv_mmscmd_mul = 1 .
 4749        IF gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product EQ c_prod_gas AND p_bmd IS NOT INITIA
       L.
 4750          lv_mmscmd_mul = 6290 .
 4751        ENDIF.
 4752        <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + ( gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_qty2 * lv_mmsc
       md_mul ).
 4753        UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 4754      ENDIF.
 4755    ENDLOOP.
 4756
 4757  * Now dividing by no of days in case of per day units
 4758    IF p_bbd IS NOT INITIAL OR
 4759       p_tmd IS NOT INITIAL OR
 4760       p_bmd IS NOT INITIAL .
 4761      LOOP AT <gfs_sec2_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line> .
 4762        DO .
 4763          lv_index = sy-index .
 4764          IF lv_index EQ 2.
 4765            ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_
       field> .
 4766            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 4767              lv_target_name = <gfs_field> .
 4768              PERFORM get_target_from_name USING lv_target_name CHANGING l
       v_tar_code.
 4769            ENDIF.
 4770          ENDIF.
 4771          IF lv_index GT 2.
 4772            ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_
       field> .
 4773            IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
 4774              EXIT .
 4775            ENDIF.
 4776
 4777            lv_days = gv_month_end_datum - gv_month_begin_datum + 1 .
 4778            PERFORM get_target_start_date USING lv_index lv_tar_code CHANG
       ING lv_start_date .
 4779            IF lv_start_date GT gv_month_begin_datum .
 4780              lv_days =  gv_month_end_datum - lv_start_date + 1 .
 4781            ENDIF.
 4782            <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> / lv_days .
 4783            UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 4784          ENDIF.
 4785        ENDDO.
 4786      ENDLOOP .
 4787    ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page     90
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4788
 4789  ENDFORM.
 4790  FORM fill_dynamic_table_sec2b .
 4791    DATA : lv_col_name      TYPE lvc_fname,
 4792           lv_combine_field TYPE c,
 4793           lv_index         TYPE sy-tabix,
 4794           lv_target_name   TYPE char25,

 4796           lv_days          TYPE sy-tabix,
 4797           lv_start_date    TYPE sy-tabix,
 4798           lv_tar_code      TYPE zpra_t_prd_tar-tar_code.
 4799
 4800    SORT gt_zpra_t_prd_tar BY tar_code .
 4801    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_tar INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_tar .
 4802      CLEAR lv_combine_field .
 4803      AT NEW tar_code .
 4804        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <gfs_sec2_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line> .
 4805        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'COL02' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_field

       GING lv_target_name.
 4807        <gfs_field> = lv_target_name.
 4808      ENDAT .
 4809      PERFORM convert_target_units CHANGING gs_zpra_t_prd_tar.
 4810
 4811      IF gs_zpra_t_prd_tar-product = c_prod_gas.

 5379  FORM get_target_type_name  USING    p_tar_code
 5380                             CHANGING p_target_name.
 5381    CASE p_tar_code.
 5382      WHEN 'TAR_BE'.
 5383        p_target_name = 'BE' .
 5384      WHEN 'TAR_IN'.
 5385        p_target_name = 'IN' .
 5386      WHEN 'TAR_EX'.
 5387        p_target_name = 'MOU-Ex' .
 5388      WHEN 'TAR_VG'.
 5389        p_target_name = 'MOU VG' .
 5390      WHEN 'TAR_PC'.
 5391        p_target_name = 'PC' .
 5392      WHEN 'TAR_RE'.
 5393        p_target_name = 'RE' .
 5394      WHEN OTHERS.
 5395    ENDCASE.
 5396  ENDFORM.

 7254  * v2.9: Robust current-FY YTD = average of the daily sheet (<gfs_dyn_tab
       le>).
 7255  *   When the DPR daily tab spans the whole current FY (early-FY reports)
       , the
 7256  *   YTD Actual row (sec2d INDEX 1) is recomputed as the per-column avera
       ge of the
 7257  *   FY days in the daily sheet, so YTD matches MTD / the daily sheet for
        every
 7258  *   asset (oil & gas) and bypasses the rprd / 2d-pipeline day-drops & do
       uble
 7259  *   counts. Guard: only when the daily tab starts on/before the FY start
       , so the
 7260  *   last N_fy daily rows are exactly the FY days. Prior-FY row (INDEX 2)
        and
 7261  *   later-FY reports (daily tab shorter than YTD) keep the existing comp
       utation.
 7262    IF gv_month_back_datum LE gv_year_start_date.
 7263      DATA: lv_nfy     TYPE i,

 7264            lv_dtot    TYPE i,
 7265            lv_rowfrom TYPE i,
 7266            lv_ccol    TYPE i,

 7269      FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_ytd_line> TYPE any,
 7270                     <fs_day_line> TYPE any,
 7271                     <fs_ytd_fld>  TYPE any,
 7272                     <fs_day_fld>  TYPE any.
 7273      lv_nfy = p_date - gv_year_start_date + 1.
 7274      DESCRIBE TABLE <gfs_dyn_table> LINES lv_dtot.
 7275      IF lv_nfy GT 0 AND lv_nfy LE lv_dtot.
 7276        READ TABLE <gfs_sec2d_table> INDEX 1 ASSIGNING <fs_ytd_line>.
 7277        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
 7278          lv_rowfrom = lv_dtot - lv_nfy + 1.
 7279          lv_ccol = 2.

 7283              EXIT.
 7284            ENDIF.

       _fld>.

 7287              EXIT.
 7288            ENDIF.

       e>.

 7295                ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_ccol OF STRUCTURE <fs_day_line> TO <fs
       _day_fld>.

 7298                ENDIF.
 7299              ENDIF.
 7300            ENDDO.
 7301            <fs_ytd_fld> = lv_acc / lv_nfy.
 7302          ENDDO.
 7303        ENDIF.
 7304      ENDIF.

 7653            PERFORM get_target_from_name USING lv_target_name CHANGING lv_
       tar_code.

       _field> .

 8141                " Apply PI to gas; also apply PI to SK non-gas (data incon
       sistency: SK
 8142                " historical oil in dly_prd also stores JV after CF conver
       sion).
 8143                IF gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product EQ c_prod_gas OR
 8144                   gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset CS 'SK' OR
 8145                   gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset CS 'SAKH'.
 8146                  CLEAR gs_zpra_t_prd_pi .
 8147                  LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_pi_3f INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_pi
 8148                    WHERE asset   EQ gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
 8149                      AND block   EQ gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-block
 8150                      AND vld_frm LE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
 8151                      AND vld_to  GE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date .
 8152                    EXIT .
 8153                  ENDLOOP .
 8154                  IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL .
 8155                    LOOP AT gt_zpra_t_prd_pi_3f INTO gs_zpra_t_prd_pi
 8156                      WHERE asset EQ gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset .
 8157                      EXIT .
 8158                    ENDLOOP .
 8159                  ENDIF .
 8160                  IF gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi IS NOT INITIAL .
 8161                    gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-pro
       d_vl_qty1 * gs_zpra_t_prd_pi-pi / 100 .
 8162                  ENDIF .
 8163                ENDIF.
 8164
 8165                IF gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product EQ c_prod_gas.
 8166                  READ TABLE gt_zdpr_gas_combine INTO gs_zdpr_gas_combine
       WITH KEY asset = gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset BINARY SEARCH .
 8167                  IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 8168                    lv_combine_field = 'X' .
 8169                  ENDIF.
 8170                ENDIF.
 8171  *           Individual Column..
 8172                gv_len = strlen( gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product ) .
 8173                IF lv_combine_field IS INITIAL.
 8174                  CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' gs_zpr
       a_t_dly_prd-asset INTO lv_col_name .
 8175                ELSE.
 8176                  CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' 'COMBI
       NE'               INTO lv_col_name .
 8177                ENDIF.
 8178                ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> T
       O <gfs_field> .
 8179                IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 8180                  <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qt
       y1.
 8181                  UNASSIGN <gfs_field> .
 8182                ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    156
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 8183  *           Product Total..
 8184                CONCATENATE gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-product(gv_len) '-' 'TOTAL'
                         INTO lv_col_name .
 8185                ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> T
       O <gfs_field> .
 8186                IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 8187                  <gfs_field> = <gfs_field> + gs_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qt
       y1.

 8196                ENDIF.
 8197              ENDLOOP.
 8198            ENDIF.
 8199          ENDIF.

 8202          ENDIF.

       RATED BY '-' .

 8226          ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs

 8229            gv_e_row = gv_sec2f_start_row .
 8230            gv_s_col = gs_product_col-e_col .
 8231            gv_e_col = gs_product_col-e_col .

         .

 8463  FORM get_days_in_period  USING    p_gjahr
 8464                                    p_monat
 8465                           CHANGING p_days.
 8466
 8467    DATA : lv_poper TYPE t009b-poper .
 8468    lv_poper = p_monat .
 8469
 8470    CALL FUNCTION 'FIRST_DAY_IN_PERIOD_GET'
 8471      EXPORTING
 8472        i_gjahr        = p_gjahr
 8473  *     I_MONMIT       = 00
 8474        i_periv        = gv_periv
 8475        i_poper        = lv_poper
 8476      IMPORTING
 8477        e_date         = gv_search_begin_datum
 8478      EXCEPTIONS
 8479        input_false    = 1
 8480        t009_notfound  = 2
 8481        t009b_notfound = 3
 8482        OTHERS         = 4.
 8483    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 8484      MESSAGE 'Internal Error' TYPE 'E' .
 8485    ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    162
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 8486
 8487    CALL FUNCTION 'LAST_DAY_IN_PERIOD_GET'
 8488      EXPORTING
 8489        i_gjahr        = p_gjahr
 8490  *     I_MONMIT       = 00
 8491        i_periv        = gv_periv
 8492        i_poper        = lv_poper
 8493      IMPORTING
 8494        e_date         = gv_search_end_datum
 8495      EXCEPTIONS
 8496        input_false    = 1
 8497        t009_notfound  = 2
 8498        t009b_notfound = 3
 8499        OTHERS         = 4.
 8500    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 8501      MESSAGE 'Internal Error' TYPE 'E' .
 8502    ENDIF.
 8503
 8504    p_days = gv_search_end_datum - gv_search_begin_datum + 1 .
 8505
 8506  ENDFORM.

 8511  FORM display_section3_header_1 .
 8512
 8513    gv_row = gv_row + 1 .
 8514    gv_sec3_h_start_row = gv_row .
 8515
 8516    gv_s_row = gv_row .

 8518    gv_e_row = gv_s_row .
 8519    gv_e_col = 2 .

 8522
 8523    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Merge' .
 8524    PERFORM set_range USING 'Consortium Level' 0.
 8525
 8526    gv_s_row = gv_row .
 8527    gv_s_col = 3 .
 8528    gv_e_row = gv_s_row .
 8529    gv_e_col = gv_table_columns .
 8530
 8531    PERFORM select_range USING gv_s_row gv_s_col gv_e_row gv_e_col  .
 8532
 8533    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Merge' .
 8534    PERFORM set_range USING 'ONGC Videsh' 0.
 8535
 8536    PERFORM select_range USING gv_s_row 1 gv_e_row gv_e_col  .
 8537

 9302  FORM fill_dynamic_table_sec5a .
 9303    DATA : lv_date           TYPE sy-datum,
 9304           lv_grand_total_be TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 7,
 9305           lv_act_prod       TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 7,
 9306           lv_index          TYPE i,
 9307           lv_col_name       TYPE lvc_fname.
 9308
 9309    " Build component name via variable - literal 'GRAND-TOTAL' fails in A
       SSIGN COMPONENT
 9310    " because ABAP treats the hyphen as a path separator
 9311    CONCATENATE 'GRAND' '-' 'TOTAL' INTO lv_col_name.
 9312
 9313    " Read flat BE target (F48 = Total BOEPD of BE row) from sec2b INDEX 1
 9314    READ TABLE <gfs_sec2b_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line2> INDEX 1.
 9315    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 9316      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line2> TO <gfs_fi
       eld2>.
 9317      IF <gfs_field2> IS ASSIGNED.
 9318        lv_grand_total_be = <gfs_field2>.
 9319        UNASSIGN <gfs_field2>.
 9320      ENDIF.
 9321    ENDIF.
 9322
 9323    " One chart row per date from DPR tab-1 start (gv_month_back_datum) to
        p_date
 9324    " <gfs_dyn_table> rows start at gv_month_back_datum; index = date - gv
       _month_back_datum + 1
 9325    lv_date = gv_month_back_datum.
 9326    DO.
 9327      IF lv_date GT p_date.
 9328        EXIT.
 9329      ENDIF.
 9330      lv_index = lv_date - gv_month_back_datum + 1.
 9331
 9332      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <gfs_sec5a_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line>.
 9333      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'PRODUCTION_DATE' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <g
       fs_field>.
 9334      CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_SDATE_OUTPUT'
 9335        EXPORTING  input  = lv_date
 9336        IMPORTING  output = <gfs_field>.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    177
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 9337      UNASSIGN <gfs_field>.
 9338
 9339      " ACT_PRODUCTION = col AG (GRAND-TOTAL) for this date in main DPR ta
       ble
 9340      IF lv_index GT 0.
 9341        READ TABLE <gfs_dyn_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line2> INDEX lv_inde
       x.
 9342        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 9343          ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_col_name OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line2> TO <gf
       s_field2>.
 9344          IF <gfs_field2> IS ASSIGNED.
 9345            lv_act_prod = <gfs_field2>.
 9346            ASSIGN COMPONENT 2 OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_field>.
 9347            IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 9348              <gfs_field> = lv_act_prod.
 9349              UNASSIGN <gfs_field>.
 9350            ENDIF.
 9351            UNASSIGN <gfs_field2>.
 9352          ENDIF.
 9353        ENDIF.
 9354      ENDIF.
 9355
 9356      IF p_t_be IS NOT INITIAL.
 9357        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'TAR_BE' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <gfs_fiel
       d>.
 9358        IF <gfs_field> IS ASSIGNED.
 9359          <gfs_field> = lv_grand_total_be.
 9360          UNASSIGN <gfs_field>.
 9361        ENDIF.
 9362      ENDIF.
 9363
 9364      lv_date = lv_date + 1.
 9365    ENDDO.
 9366  ENDFORM.

 9615  FORM get_achievement_6c .
 9616    DATA lv_index TYPE sy-tabix .
 9617    DATA: lv_num TYPE p length 16 DECIMALS 10.
 9618    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_field1> ,
 9619                    <lfs_field2> ,
 9620                    <lfs_field3> .
 9621    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <gfs_sec6_table> ASSIGNING <gfs_dyn_line3> .
 9622
 9623    ASSIGN COMPONENT 1 OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line3> TO <lfs_field3> .
 9624    ASSIGN COMPONENT 'COL02' OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line> TO <lfs_field1> .
 9625
 9626    CONCATENATE '% Achv w.r.t.' <lfs_field1> 'Target' INTO <lfs_field3> SE
       PARATED BY space .
 9627
 9628    DO 7 TIMES.
 9629      lv_index = sy-index .
 9630      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line3> TO <lfs_field
       3> .
 9631      IF lv_index = 1.
 9632      ELSEIF lv_index = 2 OR lv_index = 4 OR lv_index = 6.
 9633  *     v2.8: Annual % Achv left blank - no full-year actual yet for curre
       nt FY
 9634        CLEAR <lfs_field3> .
 9635      ELSE.
 9636        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line1> TO <lfs_fie
       ld1> .
 9637  * Begin of changes by Arnav on 24.03.2026
 9638        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line2> TO <lfs_fie
       ld2>.
 9639        IF <lfs_field2> IS ASSIGNED.
 9640          lv_num = <lfs_field2>.  " implicit conversion
 9641        IF lv_num <> 0.
 9642          <lfs_field3> = <lfs_field1> / lv_num * 100.
 9643        ENDIF.
 9644        ENDIF.
 9645
 9646  *      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <gfs_dyn_line2> TO <lfs_fi
       eld2> .
 9647  *      CONDENSE: <lfs_field2>.
 9648  *      IF <lfs_field2> IS NOT INITIAL and <lfs_field2> <> '0'.
 9649  *        <lfs_field3> = <lfs_field1> / <lfs_field2> * 100 .
 9650  *      ENDIF.
 9651
 9652  * End of changes by Arnav on 24.03.2026
 9653
 9654      ENDIF.
 9655    ENDDO.

 9656  ENDFORM .
 9657  FORM fetch_data_section5a .
 9658    DATA : lv_gjahr TYPE zpra_t_mrec_prd-gjahr .
 9659    SELECT *
 9660      FROM zpra_t_dly_prd
 9661      INTO TABLE gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a
 9662       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
 9663     WHERE asset EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 9664       AND block EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-block
 9665       AND product EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
 9666       AND prd_vl_type IN r_prd_vl_type[]
 9667       AND production_date GE gv_year_start_date
 9668       AND production_date LE p_date .
 9669
 9670    PERFORM populate_no_data_entries TABLES gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a USING gv_
       year_start_date p_date .
 9671
 9672    SORT gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a BY production_date product asset block  .
 9673
 9674    lv_gjahr = gv_current_gjahr - 3 .
 9675    SELECT gjahr
 9676           monat
 9677           asset
 9678           block
 9679           product
 9680           prd_vl_type
 9681           prod_vl_qty1
 9682           prod_vl_uom1
 9683           prod_vl_qty2
 9684           prod_vl_uom2
 9685      FROM zpra_t_mrec_prd
 9686      INTO TABLE gt_zpra_t_mrec_prd_5a
 9687       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
 9688     WHERE asset        EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 9689       AND block        EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-block
 9690       AND product      EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
 9691       AND prd_vl_type  IN r_prd_vl_type[]
 9692       AND gjahr        GE lv_gjahr
 9693       AND gjahr        LE gv_current_gjahr .
 9694
 9695    SORT gt_zpra_t_mrec_prd_5a BY product ASCENDING asset ASCENDING block
        ASCENDING prd_vl_type ASCENDING gjahr DESCENDING monat DESCENDING .
 9696
 9697
 9698    SELECT asset
 9699          block
 9700          vld_frm
 9701          vld_to
 9702          pi
 9703          prod_start_date
 9704     FROM zpra_t_prd_pi
 9705     INTO TABLE gt_zpra_t_prd_pi_5a
 9706      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
 9707    WHERE asset EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 9708      AND block EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-block
 9709      AND vld_frm LE p_date
 9710      AND vld_to  GE gv_year_start_date .
 9711
 9712    SELECT *
 9713      FROM zpra_t_tar_cf
 9714      INTO TABLE gt_zpra_t_tar_cf_5a
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    184
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 9715       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_zpra_c_prd_prof
 9716     WHERE gjahr EQ gv_current_gjahr
 9717       AND asset EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
 9718       AND block EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-block
 9719       AND product EQ gt_zpra_c_prd_prof-product .
 9720    SORT gt_zpra_t_tar_cf_5a BY gjahr product asset block .
 9721  ENDFORM.
 9722  FORM process_gas_records_5a .
 9723
 9724    DATA : lt_zpra_t_dly_prd  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zpra_t_dly_prd  .
 9725
 9726    DATA : ls_zpra_t_dly_prd   TYPE                  zpra_t_dly_prd  .
 9727    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs_dly_prd> TYPE zpra_t_dly_prd  .
 9728
 9729    DATA : lv_index TYPE sy-tabix .
 9730    lt_zpra_t_dly_prd  = gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a .
 9731    SORT  lt_zpra_t_dly_prd BY production_date product asset block prd_vl_
       type .
 9732
 9733    DELETE gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a  WHERE product EQ c_prod_gas .
 9734    DELETE lt_zpra_t_dly_prd     WHERE product NE c_prod_gas .
 9735    CHECK lt_zpra_t_dly_prd IS NOT INITIAL.
 9736
 9737    LOOP AT lt_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO ls_zpra_t_dly_prd .
 9738      lv_index = sy-tabix .
 9739      IF lv_index EQ 1 OR
 9740        <fs_dly_prd>-production_date NE ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date
        OR
 9741        <fs_dly_prd>-product         NE ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-product
        OR
 9742        <fs_dly_prd>-asset           NE ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset
        OR
 9743        <fs_dly_prd>-block           NE ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-block .
 9744
 9745        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a ASSIGNING <fs_dly_prd>
        .
 9746        <fs_dly_prd>-mandt           = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-mandt .
 9747        <fs_dly_prd>-production_date = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date .
 9748        <fs_dly_prd>-product         = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-product .
 9749        <fs_dly_prd>-asset           = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset .
 9750        <fs_dly_prd>-block           = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-block .
 9751        <fs_dly_prd>-prod_vl_uom1    = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_uom1 .
 9752        <fs_dly_prd>-prd_vl_type     = 'NET_PROD' .
 9753      ENDIF.
 9754      PERFORM convert_gas_units_to_bopd CHANGING ls_zpra_t_dly_prd.
 9755  *   PERFORM convert_gas_units CHANGING ls_zpra_t_dly_prd.  " v2.1: use _
       to_bopd so chart shows BOEPD
 9756      IF ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-prd_vl_type EQ 'GAS_INJ' .
 9757        ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 = ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 *
       -1 .
 9758      ENDIF.
 9759      <fs_dly_prd>-prod_vl_qty1    = <fs_dly_prd>-prod_vl_qty1 + ls_zpra_t
       _dly_prd-prod_vl_qty1 .
 9760    ENDLOOP.
 9761    SORT  gt_zpra_t_dly_prd_5a BY production_date product asset .
 9762
 9763  ENDFORM.

 9823                                  p_top

 9824                                  p_bottom .

 9843    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Borders' = go_border EXPORTING #1 = '7' .

 9847    SET PROPERTY OF go_border 'LineStyle' = '1' .
 9848
 9849    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Borders' = go_border EXPORTING #1 = '9'.
 9850    SET PROPERTY OF go_border 'LineStyle' = '1' .
 9851
 9852    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Borders' = go_border EXPORTING #1 = '10'.
 9853    SET PROPERTY OF go_border 'LineStyle' = '1' .
 9854
 9855    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Borders' = go_border EXPORTING #1 = '11'.
 9856    SET PROPERTY OF go_border 'LineStyle' = '1' .

10095  FORM add_dummy_data  TABLES   pt_zpra_t_dly_prd STRUCTURE zpra_t_dly_prd
       .
10096
10097    DATA : ls_zpra_t_dly_prd TYPE zpra_t_dly_prd .
10098    DATA : lv_date           TYPE sy-datum       .
10099
10100    lv_date = gv_month_back_datum .
10101    APPEND LINES OF gt_zpra_t_dly_prd TO pt_zpra_t_dly_prd .
10102    DO .
10103      READ TABLE pt_zpra_t_dly_prd TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY product
       ion_date = lv_date .
10104      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
10105        ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-mandt           = sy-mandt .
10106        ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date = lv_date .
10107        ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-comments        = 'DUMMYDPRDATA' .
10108        APPEND ls_zpra_t_dly_prd TO pt_zpra_t_dly_prd .
10109      ENDIF.
10110      lv_date = lv_date + 1 .
10111      IF lv_date GT p_date.
10112        EXIT .
10113      ENDIF.
10114    ENDDO.
10115    SORT pt_zpra_t_dly_prd BY production_date .
10116  ENDFORM.
