10182  FORM populate_no_data_entries  TABLES p_zpra_t_dly_prd STRUCTURE zpra_t_
       dly_prd
10183                                 USING  p_from_date p_to_date.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    192
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10184
10185    DATA : lt_zpra_c_prd_prof   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zpra_c_prd_prof
       .
10186    DATA : ls_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE                   ty_zpra_c_prd_prof,
10187           ls_zpra_t_dly_prd  TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd,
10188           ls_zpra_t_dly_prd2 TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd.
10189    DATA : lv_date  TYPE                   sy-datum,
10190           lv_date2 TYPE                   sy-datum,
10191           lv_index TYPE                   sy-tabix.
10192
10193    lt_zpra_c_prd_prof = gt_zpra_c_prd_prof .
10194    SORT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof BY product asset block .
10195    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM  lt_zpra_c_prd_prof COMPARING product
       asset .
10196
10197    SORT p_zpra_t_dly_prd BY product asset production_date block prd_vl_ty
       pe DESCENDING.
10198
10199    LOOP AT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO ls_zpra_c_prd_prof.
10200      lv_date = p_from_date .
10201      DO .
10202        IF lv_date GT p_to_date.
10203          EXIT .
10204        ENDIF.
10205        READ TABLE p_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO ls_zpra_t_dly_prd WITH KEY produc
       t         = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
10206                                                                    asset
                 = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10207                                                                    produc
       tion_date = lv_date BINARY SEARCH .
10208        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
10209          lv_date2 = lv_date - 1.
10210          READ TABLE p_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO ls_zpra_t_dly_prd WITH KEY prod
       uct         = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
10211                                                                      asse
       t           = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10212                                                                      prod
       uction_date = lv_date2 BINARY SEARCH .
10213          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL .
10214            READ TABLE p_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO ls_zpra_t_dly_prd WITH KEY pr
       oduct         = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
10215                                                                        as
       set           = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10216                                                                        pr
       oduction_date = lv_date2
10217                                                                        bl
       ock           = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-block .
10218            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
10219              lv_index = sy-tabix .
10220              LOOP AT p_zpra_t_dly_prd INTO ls_zpra_t_dly_prd FROM lv_inde
       x.
10221                IF ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-product          NE ls_zpra_c_prd_pro
       f-product OR
10222                   ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-asset            NE ls_zpra_c_prd_pro
       f-asset   OR
10223                   ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date  NE lv_date2
                 .
10224                  EXIT .
10225                ENDIF.
10226                ls_zpra_t_dly_prd-production_date = lv_date .
10227                APPEND ls_zpra_t_dly_prd TO p_zpra_t_dly_prd .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    193
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10228              ENDLOOP.
10229              SORT p_zpra_t_dly_prd BY product asset production_date block
        prd_vl_type DESCENDING.
10230            ENDIF.
10231          ENDIF.
10232        ENDIF.
10233        lv_date = lv_date + 1 .
10234      ENDDO.
10235    ENDLOOP.
10236  ENDFORM.
10237  FORM display_run_date_time .
10238    DATA : lv_date_ext TYPE char20,
10239           lv_time_ext TYPE char8.
10240
10241    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_SDATE_OUTPUT'
10242      EXPORTING
10243        input  = sy-datum
10244      IMPORTING
10245        output = lv_date_ext.
10246    CONCATENATE sy-uzeit(2) ':' sy-uzeit+2(2) ':' sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_ti
       me_ext .
10247    CONCATENATE 'Report Generated On:' lv_date_ext
10248                'At'                   lv_time_ext
10249           INTO gv_txt SEPARATED BY space .
10250
10251    gv_s_row = gv_row + 3.
10252    gv_s_col = 1 .
10253    gv_e_row = gv_s_row .
10254    gv_e_col = 5.
10255
10256    PERFORM select_range USING gv_s_row gv_s_col gv_e_row gv_e_col  .
10257    CALL METHOD OF go_range 'Merge' .
10258
10259    PERFORM set_range USING gv_txt 0.
10260    PERFORM set_range_formatting USING  0 'L' 'C' .
10261    PERFORM set_range_font  USING 9 0 .
10262
10263  ENDFORM.
10264  FORM remove_expired_blocks  TABLES   p_zpra_c_prd_prof STRUCTURE gs_zpra
       _c_prd_prof
10265                              USING    p_from_date p_to_date.
10266
10267    DATA : ls_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE ty_zpra_c_prd_prof .
10268    DATA : lv_index TYPE sy-tabix .
10269    REFRESH gt_prod_start_end_dates .
10270    SELECT asset
10271           block
10272           vld_frm
10273           vld_to
10274           prod_start_date
10275           liscense_exp_dt
10276      FROM zpra_t_prd_pi
10277      INTO TABLE gt_prod_start_end_dates
10278       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN p_zpra_c_prd_prof
10279     WHERE asset EQ p_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10280       AND block EQ p_zpra_c_prd_prof-block .
10281
10282    SORT gt_prod_start_end_dates BY asset ASCENDING block ASCENDING vld_to
        DESCENDING .
10283    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_prod_start_end_dates COMPARING asse
       t block .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
29.07.26 OCQ      Program ZPRA_DPR_REPORT_NEW1                  Page    194
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10284    LOOP AT p_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO ls_zpra_c_prd_prof.
10285      lv_index = sy-tabix .
10286      READ TABLE gt_prod_start_end_dates INTO gs_prod_start_end_dates WITH
        KEY asset = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10287
            block = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-block BINARY SEARCH .
10288      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
10289        IF gs_prod_start_end_dates-prod_start_date IS INITIAL.
10290          gs_prod_start_end_dates-prod_start_date = '00000000' .
10291        ENDIF.
10292        IF gs_prod_start_end_dates-liscense_exp_dt IS INITIAL.
10293          gs_prod_start_end_dates-liscense_exp_dt = '99991231' .
10294        ENDIF.
10295        IF gs_prod_start_end_dates-liscense_exp_dt    LT p_from_date OR
10296           gs_prod_start_end_dates-prod_start_date GT p_to_date .
10297          DELETE p_zpra_c_prd_prof INDEX lv_index .
10298        ENDIF.
10299      ENDIF.
10300    ENDLOOP.
10301  ENDFORM.
10302  FORM convert_gas_rprd_to_boe  CHANGING p_zpra_t_dly_rprd TYPE zpra_t_dly
       _rprd.
10303  * v1.9: removed * 6290 to prevent BCD overflow on narrow DB field. Form
       is
10304  *       only called from commented-out PERFORM (line ~8112), so currentl
       y dead code.
10305  *  p_zpra_t_dly_rprd-jv_rcn_vl_qty3   = p_zpra_t_dly_rprd-jv_rcn_vl_qty3
        * 6290 .
10306  *  p_zpra_t_dly_rprd-ovl_rcn_vl_qty3  = p_zpra_t_dly_rprd-ovl_rcn_vl_qty
       3 * 6290 .
10307
10308  ENDFORM.
10309  FORM get_cf_from_date  USING    p_rec_date
10310                                  p_product
10311                                  p_asset
10312                                  p_block
10313                         CHANGING p_cf.
10314
10315    DATA : lv_monat TYPE t009b-poper,
10316           lv_gjahr TYPE t009b-bdatj.

10427  FORM POPULATE_NO_DATA_ENTRIES_3F  TABLES p_zpra_t_dly_prd STRUCTURE zpra
       _t_dly_prd
10428                                 USING  p_from_date p_to_date.
10429
10430     DATA : lt_zpra_c_prd_prof   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zpra_c_prd_prof
        .
10431    DATA : ls_zpra_c_prd_prof TYPE                   ty_zpra_c_prd_prof,

10433           ls_zpra_t_dly_prd2 TYPE                   zpra_t_dly_prd.
10434    DATA : lv_date  TYPE                   sy-datum,
10435           lv_date2 TYPE                   sy-datum,
10436           lv_index TYPE                   sy-tabix.
10437
10438    lt_zpra_c_prd_prof = gt_zpra_c_prd_prof .
10439    SORT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof BY product asset block .
10440    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM  lt_zpra_c_prd_prof COMPARING product
       asset .
10441
10442    SORT p_zpra_t_dly_prd BY product asset production_date block prd_vl_ty
       pe DESCENDING.
10443
10444    LOOP AT lt_zpra_c_prd_prof INTO ls_zpra_c_prd_prof.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10445      clear GS_ZPRA_T_PRD_PI.
10446      READ TABLE GT_ZPRA_T_PRD_PI_3F INTO GS_ZPRA_T_PRD_PI WITH KEY  asset
        = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10447                                                                  block =
       ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-block .

10450        CONTINUE.
10451       ENDIF.
10452      ENDIF.
10453      lv_date = p_from_date .
10454      DO .
10455        IF lv_date GT p_to_date.
10456          EXIT .
10457        ENDIF.

       t         = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-product
10459                                                                    asset
                 = ls_zpra_c_prd_prof-asset
10460                                                                    produc
       tion_date = lv_date BINARY SEARCH .

10493  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10494  *&      Form  PREPARE_PASTE_DATA
10495  *& Generic clipboard paste preparation - works with any dynamic table
10496  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10497  FORM prepare_paste_data TABLES p_table.
10498    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_line> TYPE ANY,
10499                   <fs_fld>  TYPE ANY.
10500    DATA lv_index TYPE sy-tabix.
10501    CLEAR gs_paste.
10502    REFRESH gt_paste.
10503    LOOP AT p_table ASSIGNING <fs_line>.
10504      CLEAR gs_paste.
10505      DO.
10506        lv_index = sy-index.
10507        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_index OF STRUCTURE <fs_line> TO <fs_fld>.
10508        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
10509          IF lv_index NE 1.
10510            CONCATENATE gs_paste cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab IN
       TO gs_paste.
10511          ENDIF.
10512          CONCATENATE gs_paste <fs_fld> INTO gs_paste.
10513        ELSE.
10514          EXIT.
10515        ENDIF.
10516      ENDDO.
10517      APPEND gs_paste TO gt_paste.
10518    ENDLOOP.
10519  ENDFORM.
10520  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10521  *&      Form  CREATE_DYNAMIC_TABLE
10522  *& Creates a dynamic table from gt_dyn_fcat into the given reference
10523  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10524  FORM create_dynamic_table CHANGING p_table TYPE REF TO data.
10525    CALL METHOD cl_alv_table_create=>create_dynamic_table
10526      EXPORTING
10527        it_fieldcatalog           = gt_dyn_fcat
10528      IMPORTING
10529        ep_table                  = p_table
10530      EXCEPTIONS
10531        generate_subpool_dir_full = 1
10532        OTHERS                    = 2.
10533    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
10534      MESSAGE 'Unexpected Internal Error' TYPE 'E'.
10535    ENDIF.
10536  ENDFORM.
